| Aspect              | LR Schedulers (Sub 1.3)                                           | Adaptive Optimizers                                         |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |
| What they control   | Global base learning rate                                         | Per-parameter effective LR lunartech                        |
| When they adapt     | Between epochs (or steps)                                         | Every single gradient update lunartech                      |
| Adaptation based on | External metrics (val_loss, epoch count) machinelearningmastery   | Internal gradient statistics (momentum, variance) lunartech |
| Scope               | All parameters share same base LR                                 | Each parameter has unique LR activeloop                     |
| Examples            | ReduceLROnPlateau, 1cycle, CosineAnnealing machinelearningmastery | Adam, RMSprop, AdaGrad lunartech                            |
| Your curriculum     | ⭐ Sub 1.3 focuses HERE                                            | Not explicitly covered in Star 1                            |
| Typical usage       | callbacks=[scheduler]                                             | optimizer=Adam(...)                                         |
| Can combine?        | ✅ YES! Often used together                                        | ✅ YES! Complementary                                        | ^UeQt4PUF
